        for agent in &fed.nodes[0].engine.agents {             assert_eq!(agent.phenotype, Some(damaged_phenotype), "both parent and daughter must carry the same inherited phenotype");         }     }     /// The other half of that composition point: with `mutation`     /// configured at `probability: 1.0` (deterministic for a clean test),     /// the daughter must get `damaged_phenotype` instead of inheriting —     /// while the parent's own phenotype is left completely untouched.     #[test]     fn fission_mutation_gives_the_daughter_a_divergent_phenotype_without_touching_the_parent() {         let bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -5.0, -5.0], [40.0, 5.0, 5.0]);         let mut config = default_config();         config.agent_params = AgentParams { drift: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], diffusion_coeff: 0.0, k_elim: 0.0, k_release: 0.0 };         config.k_uptake = 0.0;         let damaged_phenotype = AgentParams { drift: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], diffusion_coeff: 0.0, k_elim: 0.5, k_release: 0.0 };         config.fission = Some(FissionParams {             rate: 1000.0,             payload_fraction: 0.5,             next_generated_id: 910_000,             mutation: Some(MutationParams { probability: 1.0, damaged_phenotype }),         });         let mut fed = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(1, bounds, config);         fed.add_agent(1, [10.0, 0.0, 0.0], 10.0); // parent phenotype stays None (healthy baseline)         let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(1);         fed.step(&mut rng);         assert_eq!(fed.total_agent_count(), 2);         let parent = fed.nodes[0].engine.agents.iter().find(|a| a.phenotype.is_none()).expect("parent should keep phenotype=None, unaffected by the daughter's mutation");         let daughter = fed.nodes[0].engine.agents.iter().find(|a| a.phenotype == Some(damaged_phenotype)).expect("daughter should have received the mutated phenotype");         assert_ne!(parent.position, [0.0; 3]); // sanity: both real agents, not defaults         assert_eq!(daughter.position, [10.0, 0.0, 0.0]);     }     /// Closes gap 2: two agents placed close together but on opposite     /// sides of a node seam must be able to fuse — the one coupling     /// mechanism that, until this revision, was strictly node-local while     /// every other cross-node mechanism (interaction, diffusion) already     /// worked across seams.     #[test]     fn cross_node_fusion_merges_agents_on_opposite_sides_of_a_seam() {         let bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -5.0, -5.0], [40.0, 5.0, 5.0]);         let mut config = default_config();         config.agent_params = AgentParams { drift: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], diffusion_coeff: 0.0, k_elim: 0.0, k_release: 0.0 };         config.k_uptake = 0.0;         config.fusion = Some(FusionParams { radius: 1.0, rate: 1000.0 }); // overwhelming probability once in range         let mut fed = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(4, bounds, config); // slabs of width 10 each: [0,10)[10,20)[20,30)[30,40]         fed.add_agent(1, [9.7, 0.0, 0.0], 6.0); // node 0, near the seam at x=10         fed.add_agent(2, [10.3, 0.0, 0.0], 4.0); // node 1, near the same seam, 0.6 apart -- within radius=1.0         let payload_before = 6.0 + 4.0;         let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(1);         fed.step(&mut rng);         assert_eq!(fed.total_agent_count(), 1, "the two agents should have fused across the node seam");         let survivor = fed.nodes.iter().flat_map(|n| n.engine.agents.iter()).next().expect("exactly one agent should remain");         assert!((survivor.payload - payload_before).abs() < 1e-9, "merged payload must equal the exact sum of the two originals: got {}", survivor.payload);     }     /// Cross-node fusion must respect the same "node pairs beyond     /// `fusion.radius` are skipped via early-break" scan phase 3's     /// cross-node interaction already established — two agents far     /// enough apart (in different, non-adjacent nodes) must NOT fuse.     #[test]     fn cross_node_fusion_does_not_merge_agents_outside_the_radius() {         let bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -5.0, -5.0], [40.0, 5.0, 5.0]);         let mut config = default_config();         config.agent_params = AgentParams { drift: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], diffusion_coeff: 0.0, k_elim: 0.0, k_release: 0.0 };         config.k_uptake = 0.0;         config.fusion = Some(FusionParams { radius: 1.0, rate: 1000.0 });         let mut fed = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(4, bounds, config);         fed.add_agent(1, [1.0, 0.0, 0.0], 6.0); // node 0, far from any seam         fed.add_agent(2, [39.0, 0.0, 0.0], 4.0); // node 3, far from any seam, and far from agent 1         let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(1);         fed.step(&mut rng);         assert_eq!(fed.total_agent_count(), 2, "agents far outside fusion.radius of each other must not merge");     }     /// Closes gap 3: two agents sharing the same local concentration but     /// with different `uptake_override` kinetics should draw visibly     /// different amounts from the field, through the real `step()`     /// wiring — not just `mitonano_core::kinetics::uptake_amount` in     /// isolation.     #[test]     fn uptake_override_actually_changes_agent_uptake_through_the_real_wiring() {         let bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -5.0, -5.0], [40.0, 5.0, 5.0]);         let mut config = default_config();         config.agent_params = AgentParams { drift: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], diffusion_coeff: 0.0, k_elim: 0.0, k_release: 0.0 };         config.k_uptake = 0.05; // node-wide default, used by the agent with no override         config.interaction = InteractionParams { radius: 0.0, k_exchange: 0.0 }; // isolate uptake -- both agents are co-located, and interaction would otherwise transfer payload between them, contaminating the comparison         let mut fed = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(1, bounds, config);         fed.nodes[0].engine.field.deposit([10.0, 0.0, 0.0], 100_000.0); // huge, roughly-constant local concentration         fed.add_agent(1, [10.0, 0.0, 0.0], 0.0); // no override -> uses node-wide linear k_uptake=0.05         fed.add_agent(2, [10.0, 0.0, 0.0], 0.0);         fed.nodes[0].engine.agents[1].uptake_override = Some(UptakeKinetics::Saturable(SaturableUptakeParams { v_max: 0.5, k_m: 1.0 })); // caps far below what linear would give here         let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(1);         for _ in 0..20 {             fed.step(&mut rng);         }         let default_uptake = fed.nodes[0].engine.agents[0].payload;         let overridden_uptake = fed.nodes[0].engine.agents[1].payload;         assert!(             overridden_uptake < default_uptake,             "saturable override (v_max=0.5) should have drawn far less than linear (k_uptake=0.05) at this huge concentration: override={overridden_uptake} default={default_uptake}"         );         assert!(overridden_uptake <= 0.5 * config.dt * 20.0 + 1e-9, "saturable uptake must never exceed v_max*dt per step, summed across all 20 steps");     }     /// Closes gap 4: a spatially-varying diffusion profile must actually     /// change how the field spreads — a barrier (D=0) placed between two     /// nodes should block cross-node diffusion exactly the way     /// `mitonano_core`'s own zero-diffusivity test proves for a single     /// field, now proven for the federated cross-node coupling path     /// specifically (phase 4's harmonic-mean-at-the-seam logic).     #[test]     fn heterogeneous_diffusion_barrier_blocks_cross_node_diffusion_exactly() {         fn barrier_profile(pos: [f64; 3]) -> f64 {             // Zero diffusivity in a thin band straddling the seam at             // x=20 (nodes are [0,20) and [20,40] for num_nodes=2); normal             // diffusivity everywhere else.             if (18.0..22.0).contains(&pos[0]) {                 0.0             } else {                 0.3             }         }         let bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -5.0, -5.0], [40.0, 5.0, 5.0]);         let mut config = default_config();         config.agent_params = AgentParams { drift: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], diffusion_coeff: 0.0, k_elim: 0.0, k_release: 0.0 };         config.k_uptake = 0.0;         config.field_grid = (10, 2, 2);         config.index_grid = (10, 2, 2);         config.heterogeneous_diffusion = Some(barrier_profile);         let mut fed = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(2, bounds, config); // slabs [0,20) and [20,40]         fed.nodes[0].engine.field.deposit([10.0, 0.0, 0.0], 1000.0); // well inside node 0, away from the barrier         let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(1);         for _ in 0..200 {             fed.step(&mut rng);         }         assert_eq!(fed.nodes[1].engine.field.total_mass(), 0.0, "a D=0 barrier straddling the seam must block all cross-node diffusion, not just reduce it");         assert!((fed.nodes[0].engine.field.total_mass() - 1000.0).abs() < 1e-6, "mass should stay entirely within node 0, conserved");     }     /// Closes gap 5: injection at a `GlobalFace` must actually add mass     /// to the field, tracked in `cumulative_injected`.     #[test]     fn boundary_condition_injects_mass_at_the_global_boundary() {         let bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -5.0, -5.0], [40.0, 5.0, 5.0]);         let mut config = default_config();         config.boundary_conditions[GlobalFace::YMax.index()] = Some(BoundaryCondition { injection_rate: Some(50.0), drain_rate: None });         let mut fed = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(1, bounds, config);         let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(1);         for _ in 0..10 {             fed.step(&mut rng);         }         let stats = fed.stats();         let expected_injected = 50.0 * config.dt * 10.0;         assert!((stats.cumulative_injected - expected_injected).abs() < 1e-9);         assert!((stats.total_field_mass - expected_injected).abs() < 1e-6, "injected mass should show up in the field (allowing for a little subsequent diffusion spread, not loss)");     }     /// The other half: `GlobalFace::XMin`/`XMax` must apply *only* to the     /// node whose x-face is genuinely the global boundary (node 0 for     /// XMin, the last node for XMax) — every other node's x-faces are     /// internal seams, and injecting there would fabricate mass at a     /// fictitious boundary.     #[test]     fn boundary_condition_on_x_face_applies_only_to_the_correct_edge_node() {         let bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -5.0, -5.0], [40.0, 5.0, 5.0]);         let mut config = default_config();         config.boundary_conditions[GlobalFace::XMin.index()] = Some(BoundaryCondition { injection_rate: Some(100.0), drain_rate: None });         let mut fed = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(4, bounds, config); // slabs [0,10)[10,20)[20,30)[30,40]         let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(1);         fed.step(&mut rng);         assert!(fed.nodes[0].engine.field.total_mass() > 0.0, "node 0 owns the true global XMin boundary and should have received the injection");         for node in &fed.nodes[1..] {             assert_eq!(node.engine.field.total_mass(), 0.0, "node {} does not own the global XMin boundary (it's an internal seam for this node) and must receive nothing", node.id);         }     }     /// Drainage must remove mass and track it in `cumulative_drained`,     /// symmetric to injection.     #[test]     fn boundary_condition_drain_removes_mass_and_is_tracked() {         let bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -5.0, -5.0], [40.0, 5.0, 5.0]);         let mut config = default_config();         config.boundary_conditions[GlobalFace::ZMax.index()] = Some(BoundaryCondition { injection_rate: None, drain_rate: Some(0.5) });         let mut fed = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(1, bounds, config);         let z_max_edge = fed.nodes[0].engine.field.bounds.max[2] - 0.1;         fed.nodes[0].engine.field.deposit([20.0, 0.0, z_max_edge], 1000.0); // near the ZMax face         let mass_before = fed.stats().total_field_mass;         let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(1);         for _ in 0..5 {             fed.step(&mut rng);         }         let stats = fed.stats();         assert!(stats.cumulative_drained > 0.0, "drainage should have removed some mass");         assert!(             (stats.total_field_mass - (mass_before - stats.cumulative_drained)).abs() < 1e-6,             "field mass lost should exactly match cumulative_drained (no elimination or uptake active in this scenario)"         );     } } ``` ## `tests/conservation.rs` ```rust //! This is the test the whole crate exists to run: does splitting the //! real physics (`mitonano_core::UnifiedEngine`) across multiple //! independently-stepped nodes, with agents actually migrating between //! them mid-simulation, still hold the same exact mass-bookkeeping //! identity `mitonano_core::unified_engine`'s own single-engine tests //! check? //! //! `total_combined_mass(t) == C0 - cumulative_eliminated(t)` — proven for //! one `UnifiedEngine` by that crate's own //! `full_system_matches_exact_bookkeeping_identity_under_maximum_complexity` //! test. This file proves the same identity holds for the *sum* across //! every node's engine and field in a real, migrating, multi-node //! cluster — motion, field release/uptake, agent-agent exchange, field //! diffusion, and cross-node migration all simultaneously active. use mitonano_core::agent::AgentParams; use mitonano_core::environment::Bounds; use mitonano_core::interaction::InteractionParams; use mitonano_core::kinetics::{SaturableUptakeParams, UptakeKinetics}; use mitonano_core::mobility::TwoStateParams; use mitonano_core::population::{FissionParams, FusionParams, MutationParams}; use mitonano_federated::{BoundaryCondition, FederatedEngineConfig, FederatedUnifiedEngine, GlobalFace}; use rand::rngs::StdRng; use rand::{Rng, SeedableRng}; /// Real physics, real migration, zero elimination: total combined mass /// (every node's agent payloads + every node's field mass) must be /// exactly conserved at every tick, the same invariant /// `mitonano_core::unified_engine`'s own /// `full_system_exactly_conserves_mass_with_zero_elimination` test /// checks for a single engine — now summed across a 5-node cluster with /// agents crossing node boundaries throughout the run. #[test] fn global_mass_is_exactly_conserved_across_a_distributed_run_with_zero_elimination() {     let global_bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -6.0, -6.0], [60.0, 6.0, 6.0]);     let config = FederatedEngineConfig {         agent_params: AgentParams { drift: [1.2, 0.05, -0.03], diffusion_coeff: 0.2, k_elim: 0.0, k_release: 0.2 },         interaction: InteractionParams { radius: 1.0, k_exchange: 0.2 },         field_diffusion_coeff: 0.12,         dt: 0.01,         k_uptake: 0.25,         field_grid: (5, 4, 4),         index_grid: (5, 4, 4),         saturable_uptake: None,         chemotaxis: None,         fission: None,         fusion: None,         two_state: None,         heterogeneous_diffusion: None,         boundary_conditions: [None; 6],     };     let mut engine = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(5, global_bounds, config); // slabs of width 12 each     let mut rng_init = StdRng::seed_from_u64(31);     for i in 0..60 {         let x = rng_init.gen_range(0.5..59.5);         let y = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let z = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let payload = rng_init.gen_range(1.0..10.0);         engine.add_agent(i, [x, y, z], payload);     }     let c0 = engine.stats().total_combined_mass; // all initial payload, zero field mass at t=0     let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(2026);     for tick in 1..=800 {         engine.step(&mut rng);         let stats = engine.stats();         let rel_err = (stats.total_combined_mass - c0).abs() / c0;         assert!(             rel_err < 1e-9,             "tick {tick}: combined mass {} vs initial {} (rel_err {rel_err})",             stats.total_combined_mass,             c0         );         assert_eq!(stats.cumulative_eliminated, 0.0, "k_elim=0.0 must mean zero elimination throughout");     }     assert!(!engine.migration_log().is_empty(), "positive x-drift across a 5-node, 60-unit-wide cluster over 800 ticks should produce cross-node migrations"); } /// Same scenario, but with real elimination active too (all four transfer /// pathways simultaneously: motion, field release/uptake, agent-agent /// exchange, and now true elimination, plus cross-node migration). No /// closed form exists for `total_combined_mass(t)` here, but the /// bookkeeping identity `total_combined_mass(t) == C0 - cumulative_eliminated(t)` /// must still hold exactly — the distributed analogue of /// `mitonano_core::unified_engine`'s own /// `full_system_matches_exact_bookkeeping_identity_under_maximum_complexity`, /// the strongest test in that crate, now proven across a real migrating /// cluster instead of one engine. #[test] fn global_bookkeeping_identity_holds_under_maximum_complexity_with_real_migration() {     let global_bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -6.0, -6.0], [60.0, 6.0, 6.0]);     let config = FederatedEngineConfig {         agent_params: AgentParams { drift: [1.5, -0.1, 0.08], diffusion_coeff: 0.25, k_elim: 0.03, k_release: 0.18 },         interaction: InteractionParams { radius: 1.2, k_exchange: 0.25 },         field_diffusion_coeff: 0.15,         dt: 0.01,         k_uptake: 0.3,         field_grid: (5, 4, 4),         index_grid: (5, 4, 4),         saturable_uptake: None,         chemotaxis: None,         fission: None,         fusion: None,         two_state: None,         heterogeneous_diffusion: None,         boundary_conditions: [None; 6],     };     let mut engine = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(5, global_bounds, config);     let mut rng_init = StdRng::seed_from_u64(53);     for i in 0..80 {         let x = rng_init.gen_range(0.5..59.5);         let y = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let z = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let payload = rng_init.gen_range(1.0..12.0);         engine.add_agent(i, [x, y, z], payload);     }     let c0 = engine.stats().total_combined_mass;     let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(4);     for tick in 1..=1000 {         engine.step(&mut rng);         let stats = engine.stats();         let expected = c0 - stats.cumulative_eliminated;         let rel_err = (stats.total_combined_mass - expected).abs() / c0;         assert!(             rel_err < 1e-9,             "tick {tick}: combined mass {} vs C0-eliminated {} (rel_err {rel_err})",             stats.total_combined_mass,             expected         );     }     let final_stats = engine.stats();     assert!(final_stats.cumulative_eliminated > 0.0, "k_elim > 0 over 1000 ticks should have eliminated some mass");     assert!(final_stats.cumulative_eliminated < c0, "cannot eliminate more mass than existed");     assert!(!engine.migration_log().is_empty(), "this scenario should also produce cross-node migrations");     assert_eq!(engine.total_agent_count(), 80, "no agent should be lost or duplicated by migration despite everything else happening simultaneously"); } /// The integration-level counterpart to `mitonano_federated`'s own unit /// tests for `rebalance` (which check it in isolation, immediately after /// construction): this checks that rebalancing composes correctly when /// interleaved with everything else this crate does simultaneously — real /// physics stepping, real cross-node interaction and diffusion, real /// migration, and real elimination, periodically interrupted by a /// rebalance — and that the same bookkeeping identity /// `total_combined_mass(t) == C0 - cumulative_eliminated(t)` survives a /// rebalance's complete replacement of every node's field and agent list /// exactly as cleanly as it survives an ordinary tick. #[test] fn global_bookkeeping_identity_survives_periodic_rebalancing_during_a_real_run() {     let global_bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -6.0, -6.0], [60.0, 6.0, 6.0]);     let config = FederatedEngineConfig {         agent_params: AgentParams { drift: [1.0, -0.05, 0.03], diffusion_coeff: 0.2, k_elim: 0.02, k_release: 0.2 },         interaction: InteractionParams { radius: 1.0, k_exchange: 0.2 },         field_diffusion_coeff: 0.12,         dt: 0.01,         k_uptake: 0.25,         field_grid: (6, 4, 4),         index_grid: (6, 4, 4),         saturable_uptake: None,         chemotaxis: None,         fission: None,         fusion: None,         two_state: None,         heterogeneous_diffusion: None,         boundary_conditions: [None; 6],     };     let mut engine = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(5, global_bounds, config);     let mut rng_init = StdRng::seed_from_u64(17);     for i in 0..60 {         // Deliberately clustered near the low end, so the cluster starts         // out badly imbalanced and rebalancing has real work to do.         let x = rng_init.gen_range(0.5..8.0);         let y = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let z = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let payload = rng_init.gen_range(1.0..10.0);         engine.add_agent(i, [x, y, z], payload);     }     let c0 = engine.stats().total_combined_mass;     let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(2027);     let mut rebalance_count = 0;     for tick in 1..=600 {         engine.step(&mut rng);         if tick % 50 == 0 {             if engine.rebalance_if_needed(1.5).is_some() {                 rebalance_count += 1;             }             // The identity must hold immediately after a rebalance too,             // not just settle back into place a few ticks later.             let stats = engine.stats();             let expected = c0 - stats.cumulative_eliminated;             let rel_err = (stats.total_combined_mass - expected).abs() / c0;             assert!(rel_err < 1e-9, "tick {tick} (post-rebalance): combined mass {} vs C0-eliminated {} (rel_err {rel_err})", stats.total_combined_mass, expected);         }         let stats = engine.stats();         let expected = c0 - stats.cumulative_eliminated;         let rel_err = (stats.total_combined_mass - expected).abs() / c0;         assert!(rel_err < 1e-9, "tick {tick}: combined mass {} vs C0-eliminated {} (rel_err {rel_err})", stats.total_combined_mass, expected);     }     assert!(rebalance_count > 0, "this scenario (clustered start, checked every 50 ticks) should have triggered at least one rebalance");     assert_eq!(engine.total_agent_count(), 60, "no agent should be lost or duplicated across the whole run, rebalances included"); } /// The final integration test: does the bookkeeping identity survive /// *everything this crate does* simultaneously — real physics, real /// cross-node interaction and diffusion, real migration, real /// elimination, periodic rebalancing, and now agents actually being /// created (fission) and destroyed (fusion) mid-run, not just moved /// around? Fission and fusion neither touch the field nor /// `cumulative_eliminated` — they only ever move payload between agents /// (split one into two, or merge two into one) — so the *same* identity /// `total_combined_mass(t) == C0 - cumulative_eliminated(t)` that held /// before fission/fusion existed must still hold exactly, even as the /// population size itself changes underneath it. #[test] fn global_bookkeeping_identity_survives_fission_and_fusion_alongside_everything_else() {     let global_bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -6.0, -6.0], [60.0, 6.0, 6.0]);     let config = FederatedEngineConfig {         agent_params: AgentParams { drift: [0.8, -0.05, 0.03], diffusion_coeff: 0.2, k_elim: 0.02, k_release: 0.2 },         interaction: InteractionParams { radius: 1.0, k_exchange: 0.2 },         field_diffusion_coeff: 0.12,         dt: 0.01,         k_uptake: 0.25,         field_grid: (6, 4, 4),         index_grid: (6, 4, 4),         saturable_uptake: None,         chemotaxis: None,         fission: Some(FissionParams { rate: 0.3, payload_fraction: 0.5, next_generated_id: 1_000_000, mutation: None }),         fusion: Some(FusionParams { radius: 1.0, rate: 0.3 }),         two_state: None,         heterogeneous_diffusion: None,         boundary_conditions: [None; 6],     };     let mut engine = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(5, global_bounds, config);     let mut rng_init = StdRng::seed_from_u64(23);     for i in 0..50 {         let x = rng_init.gen_range(0.5..15.0); // clustered, so both migration and rebalancing have real work to do         let y = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let z = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let payload = rng_init.gen_range(1.0..10.0);         engine.add_agent(i, [x, y, z], payload);     }     let c0 = engine.stats().total_combined_mass;     let count0 = engine.total_agent_count();     let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(2028);     for tick in 1..=500 {         engine.step(&mut rng);         if tick % 50 == 0 {             engine.rebalance_if_needed(1.5);         }         let stats = engine.stats();         let expected = c0 - stats.cumulative_eliminated;         let rel_err = (stats.total_combined_mass - expected).abs() / c0;         assert!(             rel_err < 1e-9,             "tick {tick}: combined mass {} vs C0-eliminated {} (rel_err {rel_err}); population count at this tick: {}",             stats.total_combined_mass,             expected,             engine.total_agent_count()         );     }     let final_count = engine.total_agent_count();     assert_ne!(final_count, count0, "with fission at rate=0.3 and fusion at rate=0.3 over 500 ticks, population should have measurably changed from its starting count");     assert!(final_count > 0, "population should not have been wiped out entirely"); } /// The final integration test: does the bookkeeping identity survive /// literally everything this crate does at once — real physics, real /// cross-node interaction and diffusion, real migration, real /// elimination, periodic rebalancing, fission, fusion, *and* two-state /// mobility switching, all active simultaneously? Two-state switching /// neither touches the field nor `cumulative_eliminated` (see /// `mitonano_core::mobility`'s own docs — bound agents still exchange /// payload with the field normally, only their *motion* is affected), so /// the identity must hold exactly here too, same as it did for /// fission/fusion alone. #[test] fn global_bookkeeping_identity_survives_two_state_kinetics_alongside_everything_else() {     let global_bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -6.0, -6.0], [60.0, 6.0, 6.0]);     let config = FederatedEngineConfig {         agent_params: AgentParams { drift: [0.7, -0.05, 0.03], diffusion_coeff: 0.2, k_elim: 0.02, k_release: 0.2 },         interaction: InteractionParams { radius: 1.0, k_exchange: 0.2 },         field_diffusion_coeff: 0.12,         dt: 0.01,         k_uptake: 0.25,         field_grid: (6, 4, 4),         index_grid: (6, 4, 4),         saturable_uptake: None,         chemotaxis: None,         fission: Some(FissionParams { rate: 0.15, payload_fraction: 0.5, next_generated_id: 2_000_000, mutation: None }),         fusion: Some(FusionParams { radius: 1.0, rate: 0.15 }),         two_state: Some(TwoStateParams { k_on: 0.5, k_off: 0.8 }),         heterogeneous_diffusion: None,         boundary_conditions: [None; 6],     };     let mut engine = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(5, global_bounds, config);     let mut rng_init = StdRng::seed_from_u64(29);     for i in 0..50 {         let x = rng_init.gen_range(0.5..15.0);         let y = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let z = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let payload = rng_init.gen_range(1.0..10.0);         engine.add_agent(i, [x, y, z], payload);     }     let c0 = engine.stats().total_combined_mass;     let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(2029);     for tick in 1..=500 {         engine.step(&mut rng);         if tick % 50 == 0 {             engine.rebalance_if_needed(1.5);         }         let stats = engine.stats();         let expected = c0 - stats.cumulative_eliminated;         let rel_err = (stats.total_combined_mass - expected).abs() / c0;         assert!(             rel_err < 1e-9,             "tick {tick}: combined mass {} vs C0-eliminated {} (rel_err {rel_err}); population count at this tick: {}",             stats.total_combined_mass,             expected,             engine.total_agent_count()         );     }     assert!(engine.total_agent_count() > 0, "population should not have been wiped out entirely");     let bound_count = engine.nodes.iter().flat_map(|n| n.engine.agents.iter()).filter(|a| a.mobility == mitonano_core::agent::MobilityState::Bound).count();     let mobile_count = engine.total_agent_count() - bound_count;     assert!(bound_count > 0 && mobile_count > 0, "with k_on/k_off both active over 500 ticks, the population should contain a genuine mix of both states, not have collapsed to all-one-state"); } /// The final, most complete integration test: does the bookkeeping /// identity survive everything this crate does at once, now including /// heterogeneous phenotypes propagating (and, via `fission.mutation`, /// occasionally diverging) through real fission events, alongside real /// physics, cross-node interaction and diffusion, migration, elimination, /// periodic rebalancing, fusion, and two-state kinetics? Heterogeneity /// itself neither touches the field nor `cumulative_eliminated` beyond /// what each agent's own resolved `AgentParams` already accounts for /// through the ordinary decay/release pathway, so the same identity that /// held before phenotypes existed must still hold exactly here, with a /// mixed population of default- and damaged-phenotype agents coexisting /// throughout. #[test] fn global_bookkeeping_identity_survives_heterogeneous_phenotypes_alongside_everything_else() {     let global_bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -6.0, -6.0], [60.0, 6.0, 6.0]);     let damaged_phenotype = AgentParams { drift: [0.8, -0.05, 0.03], diffusion_coeff: 0.5, k_elim: 0.15, k_release: 0.1 };     let config = FederatedEngineConfig {         agent_params: AgentParams { drift: [0.7, -0.05, 0.03], diffusion_coeff: 0.2, k_elim: 0.02, k_release: 0.2 },         interaction: InteractionParams { radius: 1.0, k_exchange: 0.2 },         field_diffusion_coeff: 0.12,         dt: 0.01,         k_uptake: 0.25,         field_grid: (6, 4, 4),         index_grid: (6, 4, 4),         saturable_uptake: None,         chemotaxis: None,         fission: Some(FissionParams {             rate: 0.15,             payload_fraction: 0.5,             next_generated_id: 3_000_000,             mutation: Some(MutationParams { probability: 0.3, damaged_phenotype }),         }),         fusion: Some(FusionParams { radius: 1.0, rate: 0.15 }),         two_state: Some(TwoStateParams { k_on: 0.5, k_off: 0.8 }),         heterogeneous_diffusion: None,         boundary_conditions: [None; 6],     };     let mut engine = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(5, global_bounds, config);     let mut rng_init = StdRng::seed_from_u64(31);     for i in 0..50 {         let x = rng_init.gen_range(0.5..15.0);         let y = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let z = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let payload = rng_init.gen_range(1.0..10.0);         engine.add_agent(i, [x, y, z], payload);         // Half the initial population starts with the damaged phenotype         // directly, independent of anything fission's mutation might do         // later -- a genuinely mixed population from tick 1.         if i % 2 == 0 {             let node = engine.nodes.iter_mut().find(|n| n.agent_ids.contains(&i)).unwrap();             let idx = node.agent_ids.iter().position(|&id| id == i).unwrap();             node.engine.agents[idx].phenotype = Some(damaged_phenotype);         }     }     let c0 = engine.stats().total_combined_mass;     let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(2030);     for tick in 1..=500 {         engine.step(&mut rng);         if tick % 50 == 0 {             engine.rebalance_if_needed(1.5);         }         let stats = engine.stats();         let expected = c0 - stats.cumulative_eliminated;         let rel_err = (stats.total_combined_mass - expected).abs() / c0;         assert!(             rel_err < 1e-9,             "tick {tick}: combined mass {} vs C0-eliminated {} (rel_err {rel_err}); population count at this tick: {}",             stats.total_combined_mass,             expected,             engine.total_agent_count()         );     }     assert!(engine.total_agent_count() > 0, "population should not have been wiped out entirely");     let default_count = engine.nodes.iter().flat_map(|n| n.engine.agents.iter()).filter(|a| a.phenotype.is_none()).count();     let damaged_count = engine.total_agent_count() - default_count;     assert!(         default_count > 0 && damaged_count > 0,         "with an initially-mixed population plus ongoing mutation-on-fission, the final population should still contain both default and damaged phenotypes"     ); } /// The final, most complete integration test in this crate: every gap /// closed across every revision, all active at once. Real physics, /// cross-node interaction and diffusion, migration, elimination, periodic /// rebalancing, fission (with mutation), fusion (now cross-node too — /// gap 2), heterogeneous phenotypes, per-agent uptake overrides (gap 3), /// a spatially-varying diffusion profile (gap 4), and source/sink /// boundary conditions (gap 5) — all simultaneously. /// /// With `boundary_conditions` active, `total_combined_mass` is no longer /// conserved on its own (mass now legitimately enters and leaves at the /// global domain edge) — the richer identity /// `total_combined_mass(t) == C0 + cumulative_injected(t) - /// cumulative_drained(t) - cumulative_eliminated(t)` is what must hold /// exactly instead, and this is the test that checks it, under the same /// "everything at once" conditions every other headline test in this file /// already proves the simpler identity survives. #[test] fn global_bookkeeping_identity_survives_every_gap_closed_alongside_everything_else() {     fn diffusion_profile(pos: [f64; 3]) -> f64 {         // A genuinely spatially-varying profile: higher diffusivity near         // the low-x end, lower near the high-x end -- no exact zero         // barrier here (that's separately, exactly tested elsewhere),         // just real spatial heterogeneity actually in play.         0.08 + 0.15 * (1.0 - (pos[0] / 60.0).clamp(0.0, 1.0))     }     let global_bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -6.0, -6.0], [60.0, 6.0, 6.0]);     let damaged_phenotype = AgentParams { drift: [0.8, -0.05, 0.03], diffusion_coeff: 0.5, k_elim: 0.15, k_release: 0.1 };     let mut boundary_conditions = [None; 6];     boundary_conditions[GlobalFace::YMax.index()] = Some(BoundaryCondition { injection_rate: Some(20.0), drain_rate: None });     boundary_conditions[GlobalFace::ZMin.index()] = Some(BoundaryCondition { injection_rate: None, drain_rate: Some(0.05) });     let config = FederatedEngineConfig {         agent_params: AgentParams { drift: [0.7, -0.05, 0.03], diffusion_coeff: 0.2, k_elim: 0.02, k_release: 0.2 },         interaction: InteractionParams { radius: 1.0, k_exchange: 0.2 },         field_diffusion_coeff: 0.12,         dt: 0.01,         k_uptake: 0.25,         field_grid: (6, 4, 4),         index_grid: (6, 4, 4),         saturable_uptake: None,         chemotaxis: None,         fission: Some(FissionParams {             rate: 0.1,             payload_fraction: 0.5,             next_generated_id: 5_000_000,             mutation: Some(MutationParams { probability: 0.3, damaged_phenotype }),         }),         fusion: Some(FusionParams { radius: 1.0, rate: 0.1 }),         two_state: Some(TwoStateParams { k_on: 0.5, k_off: 0.8 }),         heterogeneous_diffusion: Some(diffusion_profile),         boundary_conditions,     };     let mut engine = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(5, global_bounds, config);     let mut rng_init = StdRng::seed_from_u64(37);     for i in 0..50 {         let x = rng_init.gen_range(0.5..15.0);         let y = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let z = rng_init.gen_range(-5.5..5.5);         let payload = rng_init.gen_range(1.0..10.0);         engine.add_agent(i, [x, y, z], payload);         if i % 3 == 0 {             let node = engine.nodes.iter_mut().find(|n| n.agent_ids.contains(&i)).unwrap();             let idx = node.agent_ids.iter().position(|&id| id == i).unwrap();             node.engine.agents[idx].uptake_override = Some(UptakeKinetics::Saturable(SaturableUptakeParams { v_max: 0.4, k_m: 0.5 }));         }     }     let c0 = engine.stats().total_combined_mass;     let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(2031);     for tick in 1..=500 {         engine.step(&mut rng);         if tick % 50 == 0 {             engine.rebalance_if_needed(1.5);         }         let stats = engine.stats();         let expected = c0 + stats.cumulative_injected - stats.cumulative_drained - stats.cumulative_eliminated;         let rel_err = (stats.total_combined_mass - expected).abs() / (c0 + stats.cumulative_injected).max(c0);         assert!(             rel_err < 1e-9,             "tick {tick}: combined mass {} vs C0+injected-drained-eliminated {} (rel_err {rel_err}); population count: {}",             stats.total_combined_mass,             expected,             engine.total_agent_count()         );     }     let final_stats = engine.stats();     assert!(engine.total_agent_count() > 0, "population should not have been wiped out entirely");     assert!(final_stats.cumulative_injected > 0.0, "YMax injection over 500 ticks should have added real mass");     assert!(final_stats.cumulative_drained > 0.0, "ZMin drainage over 500 ticks should have removed real mass");     assert!(final_stats.cumulative_eliminated > 0.0, "k_elim > 0 over 500 ticks should have eliminated real mass"); } ``` ## `tests/equivalence.rs` ```rust //! These tests check something stronger than conservation: that //! splitting one physically continuous domain into multiple federated //! nodes and coupling them via `boundary_mass`/cross-node interaction //! produces the *exact same result* (to floating-point precision) as //! running the whole thing as one monolithic `UnifiedEngine` over the //! combined domain — not merely a result that happens to conserve mass. //! //! This is checkable exactly (not just approximately) because of how the //! coupling is built: both the field boundary correction and the //! cross-node agent exchange are linear, additive corrections computed //! from a single shared pre-tick snapshot — the same snapshot each //! node's own *local* pass already uses internally. A monolithic engine //! computing the same physics over the combined domain, from the same //! initial state, is doing the identical sum of the identical per-pair //! terms, just partitioned differently across function calls. See //! `lib.rs`'s module docs ("Revision history") for the full account. //! //! Each test below isolates one coupling mechanism to keep the //! equivalence exact and easy to reason about — in particular, avoiding //! any agent motion with `diffusion_coeff > 0.0`, since that consumes //! `rng` draws in an order that depends on iteration order (federated: //! per-node then per-agent; monolithic: one flat list), which would //! make the two runs diverge for reasons that have nothing to do with //! whether the coupling logic itself is correct. use mitonano_core::agent::{Agent, AgentParams}; use mitonano_core::concentration::ConcentrationField; use mitonano_core::environment::Bounds; use mitonano_core::interaction::InteractionParams; use mitonano_core::orchestration::SpatialIndex; use mitonano_core::unified_engine::UnifiedEngine; use mitonano_federated::{FederatedEngineConfig, FederatedUnifiedEngine}; use rand::rngs::StdRng; use rand::SeedableRng; /// Isolates cross-node field diffusion: zero agents at all (so /// interaction/motion play no part), a mass deposit right at a node /// seam, diffusing for many ticks. A 2-node federated split's combined /// field state must exactly match a single monolithic field of the same /// total grid resolution over the same domain. #[test] fn federated_field_diffusion_across_a_seam_exactly_matches_a_monolithic_field() {     let global_bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -4.0, -4.0], [20.0, 4.0, 4.0]);     let field_grid = (5, 4, 4); // per node: 5 cells over each 10-wide slab -> cell size 2.0 in x     let config = FederatedEngineConfig {         agent_params: AgentParams { drift: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], diffusion_coeff: 0.0, k_elim: 0.0, k_release: 0.0 },         interaction: InteractionParams { radius: 0.0, k_exchange: 0.0 },         field_diffusion_coeff: 0.25,         dt: 0.01,         k_uptake: 0.0,         field_grid,         index_grid: (1, 1, 1),         saturable_uptake: None,         chemotaxis: None,         fission: None,         fusion: None,         two_state: None,         heterogeneous_diffusion: None,         boundary_conditions: [None; 6],     };     let mut fed = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(2, global_bounds, config); // slabs [0,10) and [10,20]     // No agents added at all — pure field diffusion, no RNG involved anywhere.     fed.nodes[0].engine.field.deposit([9.5, 0.0, 0.0], 100.0); // right at the seam, node 0 side     // Monolithic reference: same domain, 10 cells in x (matching cell     // size 2.0 exactly, so the grids align cell-for-cell across the     // seam), same deposit at the same physical location.     let mut mono_field = ConcentrationField::new(global_bounds, 10, 4, 4);     mono_field.deposit([9.5, 0.0, 0.0], 100.0);     for tick in 1..=300 {         // Federated: run just the diffusion phase of `step` manually         // (no agents to advance, so calling the full `step` would do the         // same thing, but this keeps the test self-contained and         // explicit about what's being compared).         fed.step(&mut StdRng::seed_from_u64(0)); // rng unused (no agents), seed irrelevant         mono_field.step(config.dt, config.field_diffusion_coeff);         let fed_total: f64 = fed.nodes.iter().map(|n| n.engine.field.total_mass()).sum();         let mono_total = mono_field.total_mass();         assert!((fed_total - mono_total).abs() < 1e-9, "tick {tick}: total mass diverged: federated={fed_total} monolithic={mono_total}");         // Cell-for-cell comparison, not just totals: sample a grid of         // points spanning both nodes and confirm the concentration         // profile itself matches, not merely the aggregate.         for i in 0..10 {             let x = 1.0 + i as f64 * 2.0; // center of each monolithic cell             let point = [x, 0.0, 0.0];             let mono_c = mono_field.sample(point);             let fed_c = if x < 10.0 { fed.nodes[0].engine.field.sample(point) } else { fed.nodes[1].engine.field.sample(point) };             assert!(                 (mono_c - fed_c).abs() < 1e-9,                 "tick {tick}, x={x}: concentration diverged: federated={fed_c} monolithic={mono_c}"             );         }     } } /// Isolates cross-node agent-agent interaction: agents frozen in place /// (zero drift, zero diffusion_coeff, so `advance_position` consumes no /// `rng` draws at all and iteration order can't cause divergence — see /// module docs), zero field activity, only the interaction phase active. /// A 4-node federated split's agent payloads must exactly match what one /// monolithic engine produces for the same agents. #[test] fn federated_agent_interaction_across_seams_exactly_matches_a_monolithic_engine() {     let global_bounds = Bounds::new([0.0, -4.0, -4.0], [40.0, 4.0, 4.0]);     let config = FederatedEngineConfig {         agent_params: AgentParams { drift: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], diffusion_coeff: 0.0, k_elim: 0.0, k_release: 0.0 },         interaction: InteractionParams { radius: 3.0, k_exchange: 0.3 }, // radius spans across node seams (10-wide slabs)         field_diffusion_coeff: 0.0,         dt: 0.01,         k_uptake: 0.0,         field_grid: (1, 1, 1),         index_grid: (2, 1, 1),         saturable_uptake: None,         chemotaxis: None,         fission: None,         fusion: None,         two_state: None,         heterogeneous_diffusion: None,         boundary_conditions: [None; 6],     };     // Agents deliberately clustered right around every seam (x=10, 20,     // 30) so cross-node pairs are exercised, plus some mid-slab agents     // that only ever interact locally, as a mixed scenario.     let positions_payloads: Vec<([f64; 3], f64)> = vec![         ([8.0, 0.0, 0.0], 10.0),         ([9.0, 1.0, -1.0], 3.0),         ([11.0, -1.0, 1.0], 7.0),         ([12.5, 0.0, 0.0], 1.0),         ([19.0, 2.0, 0.0], 8.0),         ([21.0, -2.0, 0.0], 2.0),         ([5.0, 0.0, 0.0], 6.0), // mid-slab, local-only         ([29.0, 0.0, 0.0], 9.0),         ([31.0, 0.0, 0.0], 0.5),     ];     let mut fed = FederatedUnifiedEngine::new(4, global_bounds, config); // 10-wide slabs     for (i, (pos, payload)) in positions_payloads.iter().enumerate() {         fed.add_agent(i as u64, *pos, *payload);     }     let mono_field = ConcentrationField::new(global_bounds, 1, 1, 1);     let mono_index = SpatialIndex::new(global_bounds, 4, 1, 1);     let mono_agents: Vec<Agent> = positions_payloads.iter().map(|(p, payload)| Agent::new(*p, *payload)).collect();     let mut mono = UnifiedEngine::new(mono_agents, config.agent_params, global_bounds, mono_field, config.field_diffusion_coeff, mono_index, config.interaction, config.dt);     let mut rng_fed = StdRng::seed_from_u64(0); // unused: zero diffusion_coeff means no draws     let mut rng_mono = StdRng::seed_from_u64(0);     for tick in 1..=200 {         fed.step(&mut rng_fed);         mono.run(1, config.k_uptake, &mut rng_mono);         // Match every agent by its known starting position/payload         // identity (agents never move, so position alone identifies         // which original agent is which).         for (i, (start_pos, _)) in positions_payloads.iter().enumerate() {             let mono_payload = mono.agents[i].payload;             let fed_payload = fed                 .nodes                 .iter()                 .find_map(|n| n.agent_ids.iter().position(|&id| id == i as u64).map(|idx| n.engine.agents[idx].payload))                 .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("agent {i} (started at {start_pos:?}) not found in any federated node"));             assert!(                 (mono_payload - fed_payload).abs() < 1e-9,                 "tick {tick}, agent {i} (start {start_pos:?}): federated={fed_payload} monolithic={mono_payload}"             );         }     } } ``` # Verified test output All run with `cargo test` under rustc/cargo 1.75.0, after a clean `cargo clean` rebuild to confirm zero warnings. tests pass across all three crates, zero build warnings, on a clean rebuild.** ## How to build and run this yourself ``` mitonano_core/          # cargo new --lib, paste Cargo.toml + src/ + tests/ above mitonano_orchestrator/  # cargo new --lib, paste Cargo.toml + src/ + tests/ above mitonano_federated/     # cargo new --lib, paste Cargo.toml + src/ + tests/ above                          # (Cargo.toml's path dependency assumes the three                          #  directories are siblings, matching the layout above) cd mitonano_core && cargo test cd ../mitonano_orchestrator && cargo test cd ../mitonano_federated && cargo test ``` `mitonano_core` and `mitonano_federated` need `rand = "=0.8.5"` (and `mitonano_core` additionally `rayon = "=1.7.0"` / `rayon-core = "=1.11.0"`) pinned exactly — newer releases in that dependency graph pull in a `getrandom` version requiring Rust's 2024 edition, which this sandbox's stock rustc 1.75.0 can't parse the manifest for at all. `mitonano_orchestrator` needs `nalgebra = "0.32"`. Builds and passes on stock rustc 1.75.0. The closed-form test suite takes ~50 seconds (Monte Carlo replicate counts chosen for a very low false-failure rate, not for speed).
